VESSEL TRAFFIC SERVICE GUIDE
FOR
THE CHANGHUA WIND FARM CHANNEL

A. Foreword
This Guide describes the vessel traffic
services provided by the Changhua Wind Farm Channel Vessel Traffic Service Center
(“Changhua VTS”) and notes for navigation within the Changhua offshore navigation area,
to maintain and enhance safety and efficiency. Vessels navigating in the Changhua
offshore navigation area are strongly advised to keep this Guide on the bridge for use in
navigation and voyage planning.

B. Changhua offshore navigation area
“Changhua offshore
navigation area” refers to the waters outside the Changhua offshore wind farms (between
the Port of Taichung and the Port of Mailiao), bounded to the north by the North
Reporting Line (N) and to the south by the South Reporting Line (S). It includes the
Changhua Wind Farm Channel, precautionary areas, and the inshore traffic area off
Changhua. (See Annexed Figure 1.)

C. Changhua VTS Area
The Changhua VTS Area extends
from the North Reporting Line (N) to the South Reporting Line (S) and includes the
Changhua Wind Farm Channel and its precautionary areas. (See Annexed Figure 2.)

I.
Changhua Wind Farm Channel
A Traffic Separation Scheme (TSS) is adopted. The Channel
comprises a Northbound traffic lane, a Southbound traffic lane, a Separation zone, and
East/West traffic buffer (reserved) zones. Boundaries are as follows. (See Annexed Figure
3.)

Northbound traffic lane (width 2 NM):
Established between the Separation zone and a
line connecting:
(1) 24°09’.96N, 120°12’.42E
(5) 24°08’.52N, 120°09’.00E
(6) 23°57’.42N,
120°00’.36E
(7) 23°52’.98N, 119°59’.22E

Southbound traffic lane (width 2 NM):
Established between the Separation zone and a line connecting:
(10) 23°56’.28N,
119°52’.74E
(4) 24°14’.34N, 120°06’.72E

Separation zone (width 1 NM):
A separation zone
bounded by a line connecting:
(2) 24°12’.42N, 120°09’.24E
(8) 23°54’.78N, 119°55’.62E
(9)
23°55’.32N, 119°54’.66E
(3) 24°13’.08N, 120°08’.40E

East traffic buffer zone (East
reserved zone) (width 2.5 NM):
Between the Northbound traffic lane and a line connecting:
(12) 24°07’.26N, 120°11’.29E
(13) 23°56’.40N, 120°02’.88E
(14) 23°52’.35N, 120°01’.85E
West traffic buffer zone (West reserved zone) (width 1.5 NM):
Between the Southbound
traffic lane and a line connecting:
(15) 23°57’.03N, 119°51’.31E
(11) 24°15’.33N,
120°05’.47E

II. Precautionary areas
Precautionary areas are established at the north and
south ends of the Channel. Navigate with particular caution within these areas.

III.
Reporting lines
To monitor and serve vessels entering the Channel, six reporting lines
are established:
T Line: (24°20’.00N, 120°05’.47E) to (24°15’.33N, 120°05’.47E)
N Line:
(24°20’.00N, 120°05’.47E) to (24°20’.00N, 120°23’.00E)
C Line: (24°20’.00N, 120°23’.00E)
to (24°05’.97N, 120°23’.00E)
P Line: (23°48’.00N, 119°45’.00E) to (23°59’.83N,
119°45’.00E)
S Line: (23°48’.00N, 119°45’.00E) to (23°48’.00N, 120°05’.67E)
M Line:
(23°48’.00N, 120°05’.67E) to (23°52’.03N, 120°05’.67E)

D. Changhua VTS Reporting
Mechanisms

I. Vessels required to report
All vessels that cross or navigate within the
Changhua Wind Farm Channel shall report to Changhua VTS. R.O.C.-flag fishing vessels that
keep AIS continuously operational are exempt.

II. Communication

Keep continuous watch
on the channels designated by Changhua VTS. Use Mandarin or English and IMO Standard
Marine Communication Phrases.

VHF channels:
CH 16 — 156.800 MHz — Distress, urgency,
safety calling
CH 68 — 156.425 MHz — Reporting
CH 74 — 156.725 MHz — Promulgation of
Maritime Safety Information

DSB/SSB frequencies:
SSB — 8806 kHz — Reporting and MSI
broadcasts (backup: 4372/8803/13194 kHz)
DSB — 27.120 MHz — Reporting and MSI broadcasts
III. Reporting

Report to Changhua VTS when:
• Southbound traffic crosses Reporting Line
T, N, or C;
• Northbound traffic crosses Reporting Line P, S, or M.

If entering the
Channel other than via the reporting lines, report 1 NM before entry.

If you must cross
a traffic lane, report to Changhua VTS.

Report contents (IMO SRS format):
A — Vessel
name and call sign (R.O.C. naval/government vessels: name only)
C — Position
(latitude/longitude)
E — Course
F — Speed
P — Dangerous goods on board, class (if
applicable)
X — Other matters related to navigation safety requested or required by
Changhua VTS

IV. Surveillance authority and responsibility (Changhua VTS vs. port VTS)
Vessels departing Taichung or Mailiao and intending to enter the Channel shall report to
Changhua VTS per this Guide.

Vessels are monitored by Changhua VTS while within the
Channel. After leaving the Channel, monitoring by Changhua VTS ceases; vessels bound for
Taichung or Mailiao shall report to the respective port VTS.

Port VTS channels (see each
port’s entry guide for details):
• Port of Taichung — “Taichung VTS”: CH14 (156.700 MHz)
reporting/ship-shore/pilot comms; CH16 (156.800 MHz) distress/urgency/safety.
• Port of
Mailiao — “Mailiao Port Radio”: CH13 (156.650 MHz) ETA/ship-shore/pilot comms; CH16
(156.800 MHz) distress/urgency/safety.

E. Vessel Navigation Regulations

I. General
provisions

Comply with COLREGs (1972, as amended), especially Rule 10 (Traffic
Separation Schemes).

Keep AIS continuously operational within the Channel unless
otherwise provided herein.

Proceed in the correct lane in the general direction of
traffic flow.

Avoid crossing traffic lanes as far as practicable; if obliged, cross as
nearly as possible at right angles, without loitering or impeding other traffic.

In
addition, observe the following recommendations by vessel type.

II. Vessels engaged in
towing or pushing
Use the East and West traffic buffer (reserved) zones for navigation.
III. Offshore wind farm service vessels

Bound for wind farms west of the Channel: from
the north or south precautionary area, turn into the West traffic buffer zone, then
proceed to destination.

Bound for wind farms east of the Channel: from the north or
south precautionary area, turn into the East traffic buffer zone, then proceed to
destination. If draught and under-keel clearance permit safe passage, you may use the
inshore traffic area off Changhua to reach the eastern wind farms.

IV. Anchoring and
other operations

Avoid anchoring in the Channel or near its north/south ends. In
emergencies, report to Changhua VTS; avoid anchoring in the traffic lanes and submarine
cable areas; exhibit appropriate lights/shapes; leave promptly once the cause ceases.
Without permission of the competent authority and prior notification to the MPB, MOTC,
vessels may not conduct underwater/surface/over-water works (e.g., dredging, surveying,
including by unmanned vehicles) within the Channel. R.O.C.-flag fishing vessels engaged
in fishing are exempt.

F. Changhua Wind Farm Channel Vessel Traffic Service Center
(Changhua VTS)

I. Changhua VTS
Established in accordance with SOLAS, Changhua VTS
monitors vessels using the Channel and provides navigational warnings and related
assistance. (See Annexed Figure 2.)
Call sign: Changhua VTS
Email: owfvts@motcmpb.gov.tw
II. Competent authority
Operated and managed by the Maritime and Port Bureau (MPB), MOTC.
Duties include monitoring vessel movements in the Channel and, when necessary, notifying
maritime SAR authorities, the Coast Guard Administration (Ocean Affairs Council), and
other agencies.

III. Implementation items

Receive reports from vessels entering the
Channel.

Maintain continuous watch on VHF CH16 and working channels.

Use RADAR, AIS,
and VHF to monitor navigation within the Channel.

Provide navigation-safety information.
Warn and advise vessels when dangerous situations develop.

Issue warnings to vessels
violating this Guide.

Notify and liaise in maritime incidents.

In case of
distress/emergency/marine casualty or incident, with approval from the competent
authority, notify the vessel’s cooperating rescue/salvage operator to respond.

IV.
Actions for violations
The competent authority may request assistance from the Coast
Guard Administration to maintain order and safety where:

A vessel refuses VTS
recommendations/management and is or may be inconsistent with “innocent passage”;

A
vessel enters the Channel without reporting as required;

A vessel in the Channel fails
to transmit AIS;

A vessel poses a serious threat to navigation safety.

G. Supplemental
Provisions

I. Navigation risks in the Changhua offshore navigation area

As
northbound/southbound traffic concentrates in the Channel, traffic density is high;
maintain close situational awareness to avoid risks.

As wind farm areas are open to
passage, vessels may emerge at any section of the Channel; keep a strict lookout and
proper watchkeeping.

If, after assessing risks, the master considers navigation in the
area or Channel unsafe, consider vessel characteristics, loading, and hydrographic
conditions, select an alternative safe route, and prepare an appropriate passage plan; it
is not recommended to pass between Mudou Islet (Penghu) and the western wind farm areas
off Changhua.

II. Notices for ships in the Changhua offshore navigation area

Changhua
VTS provides safety information and general recommendations only. Nothing in this Guide
relieves the duties set out in STCW and other instruments/regulations. The master retains
ultimate responsibility for the safety of the vessel and persons on board.

Vessels not
bound for an R.O.C. port are recommended to transit via the high seas where safe.

Report
immediately to Changhua VTS any violation of this Guide, hazards to navigation, equipment
damage, or other emergencies in the area.

Government ships conducting tests/surveys or
SAR may, after notifying Changhua VTS, switch off AIS, cross the Channel, or conduct
approved operations therein; maintain awareness of nearby traffic and prevent collisions.
R.O.C. naval/government ships conducting law-enforcement or pollution-prevention missions
may, where safe, switch off AIS, cross the Channel, or enter wind farm areas; report to
Changhua VTS if it does not impede the mission; maintain awareness of nearby traffic and
prevent collisions.

Annexed Figure 1 — Changhua offshore navigation area
Annexed Figure
2 — Changhua VTS Area
Annexed Figure 3 — Changhua Wind Farm Channel

NTM URL:
https://navy.mnd.gov.tw/Files/Others/25-142(?).pdf

GANJI MSA 1/2026
020913 UTC APR 2026
To all relevant units and vessels:
A ship grounding incident has been reported in the waters of Yonghe Town, Ji'an County,
Ji'an City. A notice on related matters is given as follows:
1. Grounding time: April 1, 2026.
2. Grounded vessel: Xianghai 516.
3. Grounding location: 600 meters downstream from Qiguling Ferry on the left bank.
4. Precautions:
1. All passing vessels should reduce speed, navigate cautiously, maintain a proper
lookout, actively avoid the grounded and rescue vessels, correctly identify navigational
marks and routes, and ensure VHF and other communication channels are online and
unobstructed;
2. Voluntarily comply with on-site law enforcement personnel’s instructions. If
assistance in rescue is needed, actively fulfill rescue obligations;
3. For any other special situations encountered, contact the local transportation law
enforcement agency (07968223359) for handling.
Ji'an City Comprehensive Transportation Administrative Law Enforcement Detachment
April 2, 2026

SUQIAN MSA 9/2026
151132 UTC JUL 2026
Due to the hoisting construction of the super-large bridge crossing the Beijing-Hangzhou
Grand Canal on the Weifang–Suqian High-Speed Railway, and in order to ensure the safety
of water traffic during the construction period, temporary navigation closure traffic
control shall be implemented in the operating water area. The relevant matters are hereby
announced as follows:
I. Control Period
6:00 to 10:00 on July 17, 2026;
6:00 to 10:00 on July 20, 2026;
6:00 to 10:00 on July 22, 2026;
6:00 to 10:00 on July 24, 2026;
6:00 to 10:00 on July 27, 2026;
6:00 to 10:00 on July 29, 2026;
6:00 to 10:00 on July 31, 2026;
6:00 to 10:00 on August 3, 2026;
6:00 to 10:00 on August 5, 2026;
6:00 to 10:00 on August 7, 2026;
6:00 to 10:00 on August 10, 2026;
6:00 to 10:00 on August 12, 2026.
II. Controlled Water Area
The waters within 1,500 meters upstream and downstream of the super-large bridge crossing
the Beijing-Hangzhou Grand Canal on the Weifang–Suqian High-Speed Railway.
III. Controlled Targets
All vessels intending to pass through the above controlled water area.
IV. Control Requirements
1. During the control period, the controlled water area shall be closed to navigation,
and all vessels are prohibited from passing. Enforcement boats of the Suqian
Comprehensive Administrative Law Enforcement Detachment for Transportation, the
navigation administration boat of the Suqian Navigation Center of the Northern Jiangsu
Waterway Administration Office, and the construction patrol boat will carry out warning
and control work in the upstream and downstream areas of the controlled water area. At
the same time, the Suqian Navigation Center will suspend dispatching of downstream
vessels at Suqian Ship Lock and upstream vessels at Liu Laojian Ship Lock, and the Suqian
Ship-Liu Canal Waterway Ship Lock Management Center will suspend dispatching of upstream
vessels at Shuyu Ship Lock.
2. During the navigation closure control period, vessels entering the section between
Suqian Ship Lock and Liu Laojian Ship Lock shall choose nearby safe waters to berth in an
orderly manner, and all vessels during the control period must obey the instructions of
on-site traffic law enforcement personnel.
3. No separate notice will be given when navigation resumes after the control ends.
Suqian Comprehensive Administrative Law Enforcement Detachment for Transportation
July 14, 2026

YUEYANG MSA 11/2026
171804 UTC AUG 2026
Yueyang Navigation Warning 11/26, to all relevant units and vessels: The towing
arrangements for the vessel “China Fishery Administration 36064” are hereby announced as
follows:
1. Towing period
From August 13, 2026 to August 14, 2026.
2. Overview of the towing convoy
Tow vessel: “China Fishery Administration 36064”, length 40 meters, beam 12.8 meters,
molded depth 1.9 meters, full-load draft 0.8 meters, air draft 10.1 meters.
Tug: “Xiang Yue Gong 023”.
Towing method: “Xiang Yue Gong 023” will push “China Fishery Administration 36064” to
form a towing convoy.
3. Water areas to be transited
From the Qilishan water area in Yueyang (approximately 3.5 km along the Xiangjiang
waterway) to the water area opposite the Hanjiang River mouth on the Wuqiao waterway in
Wuhan (approximately 1043.3 km along the lower Yangtze River waterway).
4. Matters requiring attention
(1) The towing convoy shall strictly comply with the “Navigation Rules for Vessels on the
Yangtze River Mainline from Anhui to Sichuan” and relevant laws and regulations,
implement all navigational safety assurance measures, and promptly report vessel
movements to the VTS center in the waters transited.
(2) The towing convoy shall display signals as required, keep watch on the relevant
channels, and broadcast vessel movements.
(3) Passing vessels shall strengthen lookout, pay attention to identification, closely
monitor the movements of the towing convoy, strengthen communication with the towing
convoy, and pass with caution.
(4) No separate notice will be issued after the towing task is completed.
Yueyang Maritime Safety Administration
August 12, 2026

191417 UTC AUG 2026
Luzhou Navigation Warning 24/26, Yangtze River, according to the hydrological forecast
issued by Luzhou Hydrological Center at 0800 on August 19: the water level at Luzhou
Station on the Yangtze River will be 233.27 meters (8.77 meters) at 0800 on August 20,
with a large amount of floating debris on the river surface. Accordingly, Luzhou Maritime
Safety Administration implemented temporary traffic control on the waters from Jieshipan
to Wangyemiao within its jurisdiction (Yangtze River upstream waterway mileage 824.2 km -
953 km) at 1400 on August 19, 2026, prohibiting all vessels from passing through this
water area. Relevant vessels are requested to anchor in a safe water area in advance and
follow the on-site instructions of the local maritime management authority to ensure
safety. The time for lifting the control will be determined by the local maritime
management authority according to actual conditions, and no further notice will be given.

ANQING MSA 29/2026
081102 UTC AUG 2026
Anqing Navigation Warning 29/26, Yangtze River, all relevant units and vessels:
According to the forecast of the meteorological department, affected by Typhoon No. 13
“White Dolphin”, the middle and lower reaches of the Yangtze River trunk line will
experience heavy rain and strong winds, with maximum wind force reaching force 9, which
will seriously affect the safety of vessel navigation. In order to ensure the safety of
vessels against the typhoon, Anqing Maritime Safety Administration has decided that,
starting from 1200 hours on August 8, 2026, all vessels are prohibited from proceeding
downstream into the waters below the line connecting the cone-shaped shore mark at
Madangzui and the can-shaped shore mark at Madangshan, and vessels from branches such as
the Zhongyang Ship Lock of the Yangtze River-to-Huaihe Diversion Project, Wanhekou, and
Qiupu River are prohibited from entering the Yangtze River trunk line.   Please arrange
loading and navigation plans reasonably and choose safe waters to anchor and take shelter
from the wind. Any temporary traffic control adjustment or lifting will be announced
separately.
Anqing Maritime Safety Administration
August 8, 2026



Attachment List:
Navigation Warning on the Implementation of Temporary Traffic Control for Prevention of
Typhoon No. 13 “White Dolphin” (Anhai Navigation Warning [2026] No. 0029).doc

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

WUHU MSA 29/2026
131011 UTC AUG 2026
Wuhu Navigation Warning No. 29/26, Yangtze River: According to the forecast of the
meteorological department, affected by Typhoon No. 13 “White Dolphin,” the middle and
lower reaches of the Yangtze River main stream will experience heavy rain and strong
winds, with maximum wind force reaching Force 9, which will seriously affect the safety
of vessel navigation. To ensure the safety of vessels against the typhoon, Wuhu Maritime
Safety Administration has decided that, starting from 1800 hours on August 7, 2026, all
vessels are prohibited from proceeding downstream into the waters below the line
connecting Left Bank 244# Baifu and Right Bank Wubugou in the Datong Waterway, and
vessels from tributaries such as Yuxikou Ship Lock, Qingyi River, and Zhang River are
prohibited from entering the Yangtze River main stream. All vessels are requested to
reasonably arrange loading and navigation plans and choose safe waters for anchoring and
sheltering from the wind. Any adjustment to or lifting of the temporary traffic control
will be announced separately.

Anqing Navigation Warning 30/26, relevant units and vessels: The following matters
concerning the waterborne trial activities of the vessel “Hexing 7” are hereby notified:
1. Time of waterborne trial navigation: 0800-1200 on August 19, 2026.
2. Overview of the trial vessel
Trial vessel: “Hexing 7”; navigation area: offshore, sailing in B-class ice area.
Single-engine, single-propeller, single-rudder driven, steel welded transport vessel;
when calculating containers, 20ft standard containers (6058x2438x2591) are used, with a
quantity of 860 TEU, including 20 refrigerated containers, and 324 TEU of dangerous goods
containers. Maximum length 126.90 m, molded breadth 22.40 m, molded depth 11.00 m,
designed draft 8.00 m.
3. Navigable waters
“Hexing 7” will carry out trial navigation activities in the waters between the #261
red-white buoy and the #264 red-white buoy in the lower section of the Taiziji Waterway
of the lower Yangtze River (Yangtze River lower waterway mileage 597 km—603 km).
4. Notes
(1) The trial navigation vessel shall display signals as required, strengthen
watchkeeping, remain on duty on the relevant channels and broadcast vessel movements, and
implement all navigational safety assurance measures.
(2) Passing vessels shall strengthen lookout, closely monitor the vessel’s movements,
maintain close communication, pass with caution, and keep as far away as possible from
the trial navigation vessel.
(3) No separate notice will be given after the trial operation is completed.
Anqing Maritime Safety Administration
August 18, 2026

Broadcast time: Anqing Information Broadcast Station, once every hour

WUHAN MSA 38/2026
061407 UTC AUG 2026
HANGZHOU MARITIME SAFETY NOTICE 38/26, To all relevant units and vessels: The following
notice is hereby issued regarding the towing operation of the vessel “Changyan 2”:
1. Towing period
From July 30, 2026 to August 6, 2026.
2. Overview of the towing convoy
Tow: “Changyan 2”, length 149.8 m, beam 22.80 m, molded depth 12.70 m, maximum height
31.9 m.
Tug: “Rescue 3001”.
Towing method: Rescue 3001 will push directly against the stern of Changyan 2 to form a
towing convoy for towing.
When departing from Wuhan Channel Comprehensive Wharf, the convoy will have a total
length of 114.5 m, total width of 20 m, draft of 1.8 m/2.6 m (bow/stern), and maximum
height above the waterline of 18 m (top height of the crane on Changyan 2).
3. Waterway to be traversed
From Wuhan Yangluo Channel Wharf (approximately 1055 km in the lower reaches of the
Yangtze River waterway) to Liuhekou (0 km in the lower reaches of the Yangtze River
waterway).
4. Matters requiring attention
(1) The towing convoy shall strictly comply with the “Navigation Rules for Vessels on the
Yangtze River Mainline from Anhui to Sichuan” and relevant laws, regulations, and other
requirements, implement all navigational safety assurance measures, and promptly report
vessel movements to the VTS center in the waters traversed.
(2) The towing convoy shall display signals as required, keep watch on the relevant
channels, and broadcast vessel movements.
(3) The towing convoy shall keep watch on the relevant channels and broadcast vessel
movements as required. Passing vessels shall strengthen communication with the towing
convoy, give way with caution, and navigate as far away from the towing convoy as
possible.
(4) No separate notice will be issued after the towing operation is completed.
Wuhan Maritime Safety Administration
July 29, 2026

HANGHAI NOTICE 39/26, To all relevant units and vessels: The following notice is hereby
issued regarding the towing operation of the vessel “ATHENIAN FIDELITY”:
1. Towing period
From August 6, 2026 to August 27, 2026
2. Overview of the towing convoy
Towage vessel: “ATHENIAN FIDELITY”, overall length 149.8 meters, molded breadth 22.80
meters, molded depth 12.70 meters.
Tugboats: “Changhang Tuo 23002” and “Changjiang Jiulao No. 1”.
Towing arrangement: A double-tug towing formation will be adopted. “Changjiang Jiulao No.
1” will serve as the main tug, positioned at the starboard aft of “ATHENIAN FIDELITY”,
responsible for propulsion and steering. “Changhang Tuo 23002” will serve as the
auxiliary tug, positioned at the port aft of “ATHENIAN FIDELITY”, assisting with
propulsion and steering. The sterns of the two tugboats extend approximately 4 meters
beyond the stern of the tow. After formation is completed, the convoy will have an
overall length of approximately 154 meters, an overall width of approximately 45 meters,
a draft of 7.70 meters forward / 7.95 meters aft, and a maximum height above the
waterline of 22.87 meters (factory departure height).
3. Waterway to be transited
From the Shuangliu Base of Wuhan Wuchang Shipbuilding Industry Group Co., Ltd.
(approximately at kilometer 993 of the lower Yangtze River waterway) to Jingjiang Chengxi
Shipyard (approximately at kilometer 138 of the lower Yangtze River waterway)
4. Matters requiring attention
(1) The towing convoy shall strictly comply with the “Navigation Rules for Ships on the
Yangtze River Trunk Line from Anhui to Sichuan” and relevant laws, regulations, and other
requirements, implement all navigational safety assurance measures, and promptly report
vessel movements to the VTS center in the waters transited.
(2) The towing convoy shall display signals as required, keep watch on the relevant
channels, and broadcast vessel movements.
(3) The towing convoy shall keep watch on the relevant channels and broadcast vessel
movements as required. Passing vessels shall strengthen communication with the towing
convoy, pass with caution, and navigate as far away from the towing convoy as possible.
(4) No separate notice will be issued upon completion of the towing operation.
Wuhan Maritime Safety Administration

Yihang Notice No. 61/26, to all relevant units and vessels, the following matters
concerning the towing of “Huayi Firefighting and Rescue Tug 1” towing “Yigang Anchor
33-10” and the “floating gate” of the Weilu Hub Project are hereby announced:
1. Towing period: July 31, 2026 to August 31, 2026.
2. Towing method: This towing operation will be conducted by a flotilla consisting of
“Huayi Firefighting and Rescue Tug 1,” “Yigang Anchor 33-10,” and the “floating gate,” in
a starboard-side formation.
3. Overall dimensions of the flotilla after formation: total length 91 m, total width 21
m, maximum draft 4 m, and maximum height above the waterline 14.8 m.
4. Waterway to be transited: from the Yichang Gezhouba Shipyard Wharf to Yonghezhou
Anchorage in the Hukou waterway of Jiujiang.
5. Precautions:
(1) The towing flotilla shall display signals as required, strengthen watchkeeping,
remain on duty on the relevant channels, and broadcast vessel movements;
(2) Passing vessels shall strengthen lookout, closely monitor the movements of the towing
flotilla, and keep as far away from it as possible;
(3) No separate notice will be issued upon completion of the towing operation.

YIBIN MSA 62/2026
061657 UTC AUG 2026
Yibin Navigation Warning No. 62/26: According to CCTV and on-site observations, some
waters within the jurisdiction are currently experiencing strong winds accompanied by
heavy rainfall. To ensure the safety of vessel navigation, the Yibin Maritime Safety
Administration has decided to implement temporary traffic control from 14:20 on August 6,
2026, on the Yangtze River mainline from the Yibin Jinsha River Chengdu–Guiyang
High-Speed Railway Bridge to Jiang'an Wangyemiao (Yangtze River upper reaches waterway
mileage 1050.30 km - 952.40 km), and on the Min River from the Neikun Railway Bridge to
the Min River Hejiangmen waters. All relevant units and vessels are requested to: adjust
navigation plans as early as possible and are prohibited from sailing through the above
waters; vessels currently navigating in these waters shall promptly choose a safe water
area to anchor (or moor) and shall not navigate recklessly, and shall obey the on-site
command of the maritime authority. The time for lifting the navigation prohibition and
traffic control will be notified separately.

YIBIN MSA 63/2026

Yibin Navigation Warning No. 63/26, based on CCTV and on-site observations, dense fog
with visibility less than 500 meters has occurred in the waters from Lizhuang to Xiejiaba
(Upper Yangtze River waterway mileage 1025KM-1008KM) and from Qingfuchang to Wangyemiao
(Upper Yangtze River waterway mileage 963KM-952.4KM) within our jurisdiction, which do
not meet navigational conditions. Accordingly, Yibin Maritime Safety Administration has
decided to implement temporary traffic control over the above-mentioned waters starting
from 06:00 on August 9, 2026. All relevant units and vessels are requested to adjust
navigation plans as early as possible and are prohibited from sailing through the above
waters; vessels currently navigating in these waters shall, as soon as possible, choose a
safe water area to anchor (moor) and shall not sail in fog, and shall obey the on-site
command of the maritime authority. The time for lifting the navigation prohibition and
traffic control will be notified separately.

convoy, and keep as far away from the towing convoy as possible;

YIBIN MSA 64/2026
191022 UTC AUG 2026
Yibin Maritime Navigation Warning No. 64/26, Yibin Maritime Safety Administration
Navigation Warning: According to forecasts by the hydrological department, affected by
inflow from the upper reaches of the Minjiang River, the flood peak is expected to arrive
at Hejiangmen at 19:00 on August 19, 2026, with the water level at Hejiangmen reaching
11.16 meters. Based on CCTV and on-site observations, the current flow velocity within
the jurisdictional waterway is relatively fast and there are many floating objects. To
ensure the safe navigation of vessels, Yibin Maritime Safety Administration has decided
to implement temporary navigation prohibition traffic control in the waters below the
Chengdu-Guiyang High-Speed Railway Bridge on the Jinsha River and below the Neikun
Railway Bridge on the Minjiang River to Wangyemiao in Jiang'an, effective from 10:00 on
August 19, 2026. Precautions: (1) During the navigation prohibition period, except for
emergency and official vessels, vessels subject to traffic control shall adjust their
navigation plans in advance, are strictly prohibited from navigating in the controlled
waters, and shall berth in safe waters in advance; the time for resumption of navigation
will be notified separately. (2) During the passage of the flood peak, vessels within the
jurisdiction shall ensure adequate duty personnel, inspect in advance the main and
auxiliary engines, rudder systems, and mooring/anchoring equipment, strengthen
watchkeeping, maintain smooth communications, closely monitor changes in water level and
flow, floating objects on the river surface, and abnormal conditions of navigation aids,
and strictly prevent incidents such as dragging anchor and broken mooring lines. (3) All
relevant units, port and shipping enterprises, and vessel owners shall strengthen the
collection of flood-peak-related information, stock up on emergency supplies, and take
effective preventive measures in advance to ensure safety during the passage of the flood
peak.

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

MH 501
HYDROGRAPHIC NOTE
FORWARDING INFORMATION FOR MALAYSIAN CHARTS AND
HYDROGRAPHIC PUBLICATIONS
INSTRUCTION: -
1. MARINERS ARE REQUESTED TO NOTIFY THE NATIONAL HYDROGRAPHIC CENTRE, BANDAR ARMADA
PUTRA, PULAU INDAH, 42009 PORT KLANG,
SELANGOR, MALAYSIA, WHEN NEW OR SUSPECTED DANGERS
TO NAVIGATION ARE DISCOVERED, CHANGE OBSERVED IN AIDS TO NAVIGATION, OR CORRECTIONS TO
PUBLICATIONS SEEMS TO BE NECESSARY.
2. THIS FORM AND ITS INSTRUCTIONS HAVE BEEN DESIGNED
TO HELP BOTH THE SENDER AND THE RECIPIENT. IT SHOULD BE USED, OR FOLLOWED CLOSELY,
WHENEVER APPROPRIATE. COPIES OF THIS FORM MAY BE OBTAINED GRATIS FROM THE HYDROGRAPHIC
DIRECTORATE AT THE ABOVE ADDRESS.
3. WHEN POSITION IS DEFINED BY SEXTANT ANGLES OR
BEARINGS (TRUE OR MAGNETIC BEING SPECIFIED) MORE THAN TWO SHOULD BE USED IN ORDER TO
PROVIDE AND CHECK. DISTANCES OBSERVED BY RADAR AND THE READING OF LORAN, DECCA, ETC,
SHOULD BE QUOTED. HOWEVER, WHEN THERE IS A SERIES OF
FIXES A LONG A SHIP COURSE, ONLY THE
METHOD OF FIXING AND THE OBJECTS USED NEED BE INDICATED. LATITUDE AND LONGITUDE SHOULD
ONLY BE USED
SPECIFICALLY TO POSITION THE DETAILS WHEN THEY HAVE BEEN FIXED BY THE
ASTRONOMICAL OBSERVATIONS AND FULL DESCRIPTION OF THE LATTER SHOULD BE GIVEN.
4. A
CUTTING FROM THE LARGEST SCALE CHART IS THE BEST MEDIUM FOR FORWARDING DETAILS, THE
ALTERATIONS AND ADDITIONS BEING SHOWN THEREON IN
RED. WHEN REQUESTED, A NEW COPY WILL BE
SENT IN REPLACEMENT OF CHART THAT HAS BEEN USED TO FORWARD INFORMATION, OR WHEN EXTENSIVE
OBSERVATIONS HAVE INVOLVED DEFACEMENT OF THE OBSERVER'S CHART. IF IT IS PREFERRED TO SHOW
THE AMENDMENTS ON A TRACING OF THE LARGEST SCALE CHART
(RATHER THAN ON THE CHART ITSELF)
THESE SHOULD BE IN RED AS ABOVE, BUT ADEQUATE DETAILS FROM THE CHART MUST BE TRACED IN
BLACK INK TO ENABLE THE
AMENDMENTS TO BE FITTED CORRECTLY.
5. WHEN SOUNDINGS ARE OBTAINED
THE MARINER'S HANDBOOK (NP 100) SHOULD BE CONSULTED. THE ECHO SOUNDING TRACE SHOULD BE
MARKED
WITH TIMES, DEPTHS, ETC., AND FORWARDED WITH THE REPORT. IT IS IMPORTANT TO STATE
WHETHER THE ECHO SOUNDER IS SET TO REGISTER DEPTHS BELOW THE
SURFACE OR BELOW THE KEEL;
IN THE LATTER CASE THE VESSELS DRAUGHT SHOULD BE GIVEN. TIME AND DATE SHOULD BE GIVEN IN
ORDER THAT CORRECTIONS FOR THE
HEIGHT OF THE TIDE MAY BE MADE WHERE NECESSARY. THE MAKE,
NAME AND TYPE OF SET, TOGETHER WITH STYLUS SPEED/SOUND VELOCITY, SHOULD ALSO BE
GIVEN.
6.
MODERN ECHO SOUNDERS FREQUENTLY REGISTER GREATER DEPTHS THAN THE ADVERTISED MAXIMUM FOR
THE SET, E.G. WITH A SET WHOSE MAXIMUM IS
500 METRES, A TRACE APPEARING AT 50 METRES MAY
IN FACT BE 550 METRES OR EVEN 1050 METRES. SOUNDING RECORDED BEYOND THE SET'S NOMINAL
RANGE USUALLY BERECOGNISED BY THE FOLLOWING: -
A. THE TRACE BEING WEAKER THAN NORMAL FOR
THE DEPTH RECORDED
B. THE TRACE PASSING THROUGH THE TRANSMISSION LINE
C. THE "FEATHERY"
NATURE OF TRACE.
AS A CHECK THAT APPARENTLY SHOAL SOUNDINGS ARE NOT DUE TO ECHOES BEYOND
THE SETS NOMINAL RANGE SOUNDING SHOULD BE CONTINUED UNTIL
REASONABLE AGREEMENT WITH
CHARTED SOUNDINGS IS REACHED. HOWEVER, SOUNDINGS RECEIVE AFTER ONE OR MORE ROTATIONS OF
THE STYLUS CAN STILL BE
USEFUL AND SHOULD BE SUBMITTED IF THEY SHOW SIGNIFICANT
DIFFERENCES FROM CHARTED DEPTH.
7. REPORTS, WHICH CANNOT BE CONFIRMED OR ARE LACKING IN
CERTAIN DETAILS SHOULD BE WITHHELD. SHORTCOMINGS SHOULD BE STRESSED AND ANY
FIRM
EXPECTATION OF BEING ABLE TO CHECK THE INFORMATION ON A SUCCEEDING VOYAGE SHOULD BE
MENTIONED.
8. REPORTS OF SHOAL SOUNDINGS, UNCHARTED DANGERS AND NAVIGATIONAL AIDS OUT
ORDER SHOULD, AT THE MARINER'S DISCRETION, ALSO BE MADE BY
RADIO TO THE NEAREST COAST
RADIO STATION. THE DRAUGHT OF MODERN TANKERS IS SUCH THAT ANY UNCHARTED DEPTH UNDER 30
METRES OR 15 FATHOMS MAY BE
SUFFICIENT IMPORTANCE TO JUSTIFY A RADIO MESSAGE.
9. PORT
INFORMATION SHOULD BE FORWARDED ON FORM MH 501A TOGETHER WITH FORM MH 501. FORM MH 501A
LIST THE INFORMATION REQUIRED FOR
ADMIRALTY SAILING DIRECTIONS AND SHOULD BE USED AS AN
AIDE MEMOIRE. WHERE THEIR INSUFFICIENT SPACE ON THE FORM AN ADDITIONAL SHEET SHOULD BE
USED.
NOTE: AN ACKNOWLEDGEMENT OF RECEIPT WILL BE SENT AND THE INFORMATION THEN USED TO
THE BEST ADVANTAGE, WHICH MAY MEAN IMMEDIATE ACTION OR
INCLUSION IN REVISION IN DUE
COURSE. WHEN THE NOTICE TO MARINERS IS ISSUED, THE SENDER'S VESSEL OR NAME IS QUOTED AS
SOMETIMES HAPPENS THE
INFORMATION IS ALSO RECEIVED IN FOREIGN NOTICE TO MARINERS. FURTHER
COMMUNICATION SHOULD BE EXPECTED ONLY IF THE INFORMATION IS OF OUTSTANDING
VALUE OR HAS
UNUSUAL FEATURES.

HAINAN MSA 158/2026
121449 UTC AUG 2026
Qiong Hang Jing 158/26, South China Sea, the South China Sea fishing moratorium will end
at 1200 on August 16. The number of fishing vessels will increase significantly, and the
convergence of merchant and fishing vessels will become dense, with a marked rise in
collision risk. All vessels are to strengthen watchkeeping and lookout, enhance
communication, coordination, and avoidance with passing vessels, and prevent
merchant-fishing vessel collision accidents. For details, please refer to the
“Recommendations on Preventing Merchant-Fishing Vessel Collisions along China’s Coast.”

HYDROGRAPHIC NOTE
FORWARDING INFORMATION FOR MALAYSIAN CHARTS AND
HYDROGRAPHIC PUBLICATIONS
INSTRUCTION: -
1. MARINERS ARE REQUESTED TO NOTIFY THE NATIONAL HYDROGRAPHIC CENTRE, BANDAR ARMADA
PUTRA, PULAU INDAH, 42009 PORT KLANG,
SELANGOR, MALAYSIA, WHEN NEW OR SUSPECTED DANGERS
TO NAVIGATION ARE DISCOVERED, CHANGE OBSERVED IN AIDS TO NAVIGATION, OR CORRECTIONS TO
PUBLICATIONS SEEMS TO BE NECESSARY.
2. THIS FORM AND ITS INSTRUCTIONS HAVE BEEN DESIGNED
TO HELP BOTH THE SENDER AND THE RECIPIENT. IT SHOULD BE USED, OR FOLLOWED CLOSELY,
WHENEVER APPROPRIATE. COPIES OF THIS FORM MAY BE OBTAINED GRATIS FROM THE HYDROGRAPHIC
DIRECTORATE AT THE ABOVE ADDRESS.
3. WHEN POSITION IS DEFINED BY SEXTANT ANGLES OR
BEARINGS (TRUE OR MAGNETIC BEING SPECIFIED) MORE THAN TWO SHOULD BE USED IN ORDER TO
PROVIDE AND CHECK. DISTANCES OBSERVED BY RADAR AND THE READING OF LORAN, DECCA, ETC,
SHOULD BE QUOTED. HOWEVER, WHEN THERE IS A SERIES OF
FIXES A LONG A SHIP COURSE, ONLY THE
METHOD OF FIXING AND THE OBJECTS USED NEED BE INDICATED. LATITUDE AND LONGITUDE SHOULD
ONLY BE USED
SPECIFICALLY TO POSITION THE DETAILS WHEN THEY HAVE BEEN FIXED BY THE
ASTRONOMICAL OBSERVATIONS AND FULL DESCRIPTION OF THE LATTER SHOULD BE GIVEN.
4. A
CUTTING FROM THE LARGEST SCALE CHART IS THE BEST MEDIUM FOR FORWARDING DETAILS, THE
ALTERATIONS AND ADDITIONS BEING SHOWN THEREON IN
RED. WHEN REQUESTED, A NEW COPY WILL BE
SENT IN REPLACEMENT OF CHART THAT HAS BEEN USED TO FORWARD INFORMATION, OR WHEN EXTENSIVE
OBSERVATIONS HAVE INVOLVED DEFACEMENT OF THE OBSERVER'S CHART. IF IT IS PREFERRED TO SHOW
THE AMENDMENTS ON A TRACING OF THE LARGEST SCALE CHART
(RATHER THAN ON THE CHART ITSELF)
THESE SHOULD BE IN RED AS ABOVE, BUT ADEQUATE DETAILS FROM THE CHART MUST BE TRACED IN
BLACK INK TO ENABLE THE
AMENDMENTS TO BE FITTED CORRECTLY.
5. WHEN SOUNDINGS ARE OBTAINED
THE MARINER'S HANDBOOK (NP 100) SHOULD BE CONSULTED. THE ECHO SOUNDING TRACE SHOULD BE
MARKED
WITH TIMES, DEPTHS, ETC., AND FORWARDED WITH THE REPORT. IT IS IMPORTANT TO STATE
WHETHER THE ECHO SOUNDER IS SET TO REGISTER DEPTHS BELOW THE
SURFACE OR BELOW THE KEEL;
IN THE LATTER CASE THE VESSELS DRAUGHT SHOULD BE GIVEN. TIME AND DATE SHOULD BE GIVEN IN
ORDER THAT CORRECTIONS FOR THE
HEIGHT OF THE TIDE MAY BE MADE WHERE NECESSARY. THE MAKE,
NAME AND TYPE OF SET, TOGETHER WITH STYLUS SPEED/SOUND VELOCITY, SHOULD ALSO BE
GIVEN.
6.
MODERN ECHO SOUNDERS FREQUENTLY REGISTER GREATER DEPTHS THAN THE ADVERTISED MAXIMUM FOR
THE SET, E.G. WITH A SET WHOSE MAXIMUM IS
500 METRES, A TRACE APPEARING AT 50 METRES MAY
IN FACT BE 550 METRES OR EVEN 1050 METRES. SOUNDING RECORDED BEYOND THE SET'S NOMINAL
RANGE USUALLY BERECOGNISED BY THE FOLLOWING: -
A. THE TRACE BEING WEAKER THAN NORMAL FOR
THE DEPTH RECORDED
B. THE TRACE PASSING THROUGH THE TRANSMISSION LINE
C. THE "FEATHERY"
NATURE OF TRACE.
AS A CHECK THAT APPARENTLY SHOAL SOUNDINGS ARE NOT DUE TO ECHOES BEYOND
THE SETS NOMINAL RANGE SOUNDING SHOULD BE CONTINUED UNTIL
REASONABLE AGREEMENT WITH
CHARTED SOUNDINGS IS REACHED. HOWEVER, SOUNDINGS RECEIVE AFTER ONE OR MORE ROTATIONS OF
THE STYLUS CAN STILL BE
USEFUL AND SHOULD BE SUBMITTED IF THEY SHOW SIGNIFICANT
DIFFERENCES FROM CHARTED DEPTH.
7. REPORTS, WHICH CANNOT BE CONFIRMED OR ARE LACKING IN
CERTAIN DETAILS SHOULD BE WITHHELD. SHORTCOMINGS SHOULD BE STRESSED AND ANY
FIRM
EXPECTATION OF BEING ABLE TO CHECK THE INFORMATION ON A SUCCEEDING VOYAGE SHOULD BE
MENTIONED.
8. REPORTS OF SHOAL SOUNDINGS, UNCHARTED DANGERS AND NAVIGATIONAL AIDS OUT
ORDER SHOULD, AT THE MARINER'S DISCRETION, ALSO BE MADE BY
RADIO TO THE NEAREST COAST
RADIO STATION. THE DRAUGHT OF MODERN TANKERS IS SUCH THAT ANY UNCHARTED DEPTH UNDER 30
METRES OR 15 FATHOMS MAY
BE SUFFICIENT IMPORTANCE TO JUSTIFY A RADIO MESSAGE.
9. PORT
INFORMATION SHOULD BE FORWARDED ON FORM MH 501A TOGETHER WITH FORM MH 501. FORM MH 501A
LIST THE INFORMATION REQUIRED FOR
ADMIRALTY SAILING DIRECTIONS AND SHOULD BE USED AS AN
AIDE MEMOIRE. WHERE THEIR INSUFFICIENT SPACE ON THE FORM AN ADDITIONAL SHEET SHOULD BE
USED.
NOTE: AN ACKNOWLEDGEMENT OF RECEIPT WILL BE SENT AND THE INFORMATION THEN USED TO
THE BEST ADVANTAGE, WHICH MAY MEAN IMMEDIATE ACTION OR
INCLUSION IN REVISION IN DUE
COURSE. WHEN THE NOTICE TO MARINERS IS ISSUED, THE SENDER'S VESSEL OR NAME IS QUOTED AS
SOMETIMES HAPPENS THE
INFORMATION IS ALSO RECEIVED IN FOREIGN NOTICE TO MARINERS. FURTHER
COMMUNICATION SHOULD BE EXPECTED ONLY IF THE INFORMATION IS OF OUTSTANDING
VALUE OR HAS
UNUSUAL FEATURES.

CHONGQING MSA 239/2026
051613 UTC JUN 2026
Chongqing Navigation Warning 239/26, "The highest elevation of Fengdu Tou Outer Liang and
Er Outer Liang reefs is approximately 147 meters. When the water level drops below 152
meters in front of the dam, vessels operating in and out of Longju cargo terminal, as
well as downstream vessels, are prone to reef accidents. The warning is as follows:
1. Vessels operating in and out of Longju should avoid Fengdu Tou Outer Liang and Er
Outer Liang reefs and navigate cautiously.
2. Downstream vessels passing through the Fengdu Tou Outer Liang and Er Outer Liang
waters should raise their vessel height early to prevent reef accidents.

CHONGQING MSA 285/2026
240644 UTC JUN 2026
Yuhang Warning 285/26, all vessels please note: dense fog has appeared in the waters from
Chongqing Chaotianmen Lijiatuo Yangtze River Bridge to E'gongyan Yangtze River Bridge
(Yangtze River upstream waterway mileage 677 km-669 km), from Banan Lijihe to Zhanqizi
(Yangtze River upstream waterway mileage 723 km-716 km), and from Meijialiang to Masangxi
Yangtze River Bridge (Yangtze River upstream waterway mileage 691 km-682 km), with
visibility less than 500 meters. Chongqing Maritime Safety Administration implemented
temporary traffic control over the above waters at 0600 on June 24, prohibiting all
vessels from navigating or conducting construction operations within the controlled
waters. Vessels intending to pass through are requested to choose a safe anchorage in
advance and follow the on-site instructions of the local maritime management authority to
ensure safety. The time for lifting the control will be determined by the local maritime
management authority according to actual conditions, and no separate notice will be given.
